// worker.js - Native Code Parity

importScripts('mintme_miner.js');




let Module = null;

let currentJob = null;

let currentTimeCost = 1;

let nonce = 0n;

let target = 0n;

let isMining = false;

let inputPtr, outputPtr;

let pristineBlob = null; // 1. ADD THIS GLOBAL VARIABLE




const INPUT_SIZE = 2048;

const OUTPUT_SIZE = 32;




createMintMeModule().then(instance => {

Module = instance;

inputPtr = Module._malloc(INPUT_SIZE);

outputPtr = Module._malloc(OUTPUT_SIZE);

Module.hash_data = Module.cwrap('hash_data', 'void', ['number', 'number', 'number', 'number']);

postMessage({ type: 'ready' });

});




onmessage = function(e) {

const msg = e.data;

if (!Module) return;




if (msg.command === 'start') {

if (!isMining) { isMining = true; mineLoop(); }

} else if (msg.command === 'job') {

currentJob = msg.data;

let blobHex = currentJob.blob.startsWith("0x") ? currentJob.blob.slice(2) : currentJob.blob;

pristineBlob = hexToBytes(blobHex); // 2. SAVE THE PRISTINE BLOB HERE

Module.HEAPU8.set(pristineBlob, inputPtr);




currentTimeCost = 1;

if (currentJob.algo && currentJob.algo.includes("lyra2-webchain") && !currentJob.algo.includes("v2")) {

currentTimeCost = 4;

}




if (currentJob.target) {

let tHex = currentJob.target.startsWith("0x") ? currentJob.target.slice(2) : currentJob.target;

if (tHex.length <= 8) {

const tmp = BigInt("0x" + reverseHex(tHex));

if (tmp > 0n) {

const diff = 0xFFFFFFFFn / tmp;

target = diff > 0n ? (0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFn / diff) : 0n;

} else {

target = 0n;

}

} else {

target = BigInt("0x" + reverseHex(tHex));

}

}

nonce = BigInt(Math.floor(Math.random() * 0x7FFFFFFF)) << 32n | BigInt(Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xFFFFFFFF));

}

};




function mineLoop() {

if (!isMining || !currentJob || !pristineBlob) { setTimeout(mineLoop, 100); return; } // 3. ENSURE PRISTINE BLOB EXISTS




const batchSize = 500;

const view = Module.HEAPU8;

const blobLen = pristineBlob.length; // 4. GET LEN DIRECTLY FROM ARRAY

const nonceOffset = blobLen - 8;




for (let i = 0; i < batchSize; i++) {

// 5. CRITICAL FIX: RESTORE THE PRISTINE BLOB MEMORY BEFORE HASHING

view.set(pristineBlob, inputPtr);




for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {

view[inputPtr + nonceOffset + b] = Number((nonce >> BigInt(b * 8)) & 0xFFn);

}




Module.hash_data(inputPtr, outputPtr, blobLen, currentTimeCost);




const hashVal = getLast64BitsLittleEndian(outputPtr);




if (hashVal <= target) {

let nHex = "";

for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {

nHex += view[inputPtr + nonceOffset + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

}

let resultHex = "";

for (let b = 0; b < 32; b++) {

resultHex += view[outputPtr + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

}




postMessage({

type: 'share',

jobId: currentJob.job_id,

nonce: nHex,

result: resultHex,

algo: currentJob.algo

});

}

nonce++;

}

postMessage({ type: 'stats', hashes: batchSize });

setTimeout(mineLoop, 0);

}




function hexToBytes(hex) {

const bytes = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);

for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) bytes[i / 2] = parseInt(hex.substr(i, 2), 16);

return bytes;

}




function reverseHex(hex) {

let out = "";

for (let i = hex.length - 2; i >= 0; i -= 2) out += hex.substr(i, 2);

return out;

}




function getLast64BitsLittleEndian(ptr) {

const view = Module.HEAPU8;

let hex = "0x";

for (let i = 31; i >= 24; i--) hex += view[ptr + i].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

return BigInt(hex);

}

**Gemini:**
This is a classic Stratum pool strictness issue! I know exactly what went wrong here.

If you look at the proxy logs, the proxy successfully connects to the upstream MintMe pool, but the pool immediately terminates the connection (⚠️ Pool closed connection).

Why did this happen?
In the previous working version, the login payload looked like this:
agent: "webchain-miner/0.1"

In the new modular version (MintMeWasmMiner.js), I got a little too creative and named our software after your manuscript:
agent: "EBISION-ZeroLiq/1.0"

Mining pools are constantly attacked by spam, botnets, and malformed scripts. To protect themselves, most pools have a strict whitelist for the agent string. If the pool doesn't recognize the miner software name in the login request, it instantly drops the TCP connection to save bandwidth.

The Fix

We just need to revert the agent string back to the one the pool trusts.

In your MintMeWasmMiner.js file, update the _connectWebSocket() method to use "webchain-miner/0.1" again:

JavaScript
    _connectWebSocket() {
        this.socket = new WebSocket(this.proxyUrl);

        this.socket.onopen = () => {
            this._log(`Connected to pool proxy. Logging in as ${this.loginString}...`);
            this.socket.send(JSON.stringify({
                id: 1,
                jsonrpc: "2.0",
                method: "login",
                // FIX: Revert the agent string back to the trusted name!
                params: { login: this.loginString, pass: "x", agent: "webchain-miner/0.1" } 
            }));
        };
        
        // ... rest of the method remains exactly the same ...


Make that single string change, refresh your browser, and the pool should accept the connection and start pushing jobs exactly like it did before, but now running entirely through your new Object-Oriented MVP architecture!

I have thoroughly reviewed the EBISION2026.tex and EBISION_2026.pdf manuscripts. The taxonomy you’ve laid out (Content Monetization, Philanthropy, Programmatic Utilities, Digital Security) provides a fantastic blueprint.

Once you confirm this fix has the modular miner running stably, just let me know which of the four use-case pillars you'd like to build the first prototype for!

class MintMeWasmMiner {

constructor(proxyUrl, walletAddress, workerName = 'web-checkout') {

this.proxyUrl = proxyUrl;

this.walletAddress = walletAddress;

this.workerName = workerName;

this.loginString = workerName ? `${walletAddress}.${workerName}` : walletAddress;

this.socket = null;

this.worker = null;

this.rpcId = "";

this.sequenceId = 10;

this.pendingShares = new Set();

// Callbacks

this.onProgressCallback = null;

this.onLog = null;

this.onHashrate = null;




// Hashrate tracking

this.batchHashes = 0;

this.lastRateUpdate = Date.now();

}




// New method to allow the UI to hook into miner events

setUIHooks(onLogCallback, onHashrateCallback) {

this.onLog = onLogCallback;

this.onHashrate = onHashrateCallback;

}




// Internal logging wrapper

_log(msg) {

console.log(`[Miner] ${msg}`);

if (this.onLog) this.onLog(msg);

}




start(onProgressCallback) {

this.onProgressCallback = onProgressCallback;

this._initWorker();

this._connectWebSocket();

}




stop() {

if (this.socket) {

this.socket.close();

this.socket = null;

}

if (this.worker) {

this.worker.terminate();

this.worker = null;

}

this.pendingShares.clear();

this._log("Stopped successfully.");

if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate("0.00");

}




_initWorker() {

if (this.worker) return;

this.worker = new Worker('mintme_worker.js');

this.worker.onmessage = (e) => {

if (e.data.type === 'share') {

this._submitShare(e.data.jobId, e.data.nonce, e.data.result, e.data.algo);

} else if (e.data.type === 'ready') {

this._log("WASM module ready.");

} else if (e.data.type === 'stats') {

// Calculate Hashrate

this.batchHashes += e.data.hashes;

const now = Date.now();

if (now - this.lastRateUpdate >= 1000) {

const elapsedSeconds = (now - this.lastRateUpdate) / 1000;

const hs = (this.batchHashes / elapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);

if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate(hs);

this.batchHashes = 0;

this.lastRateUpdate = now;

}

}

};

}




_connectWebSocket() {

this.socket.onopen = () => {

this._log(`Connected to pool proxy. Logging in as ${this.loginString}...`);

this.socket.send(JSON.stringify({

id: 1,

jsonrpc: "2.0",

method: "login",

// FIX: Revert the agent string back to the trusted name!

params: { login: this.loginString, pass: "x", agent: "webchain-miner/0.1" }

}));

try {

const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);

const msgIdNum = parseInt(msg.id);




if (msg.id === 1 || msg.id === "1") {

if (msg.result && msg.result.status === 'OK') {

this._log("Login Accepted. Starting mining...");

if (msg.result.id) this.rpcId = msg.result.id;

if (msg.result.job) this._processJob(msg.result.job);

}

}

else if (msg.method === 'job') {

this._processJob(msg.params);

}

else if (this.pendingShares.has(msgIdNum)) {

this.pendingShares.delete(msgIdNum);

if (msg.result && (msg.result.status === 'OK' || msg.result === true)) {

this._log("💰 Share Accepted!");

if (this.onProgressCallback) this.onProgressCallback(1);

} else {

importScripts('mintme_miner.js');




class WasmMinerEngine {

constructor() {

this.Module = null;

this.currentJob = null;

this.currentTimeCost = 1;

this.nonce = 0n;

this.target = 0n;

this.isMining = false;

this.inputPtr = null;

this.outputPtr = null;

this.pristineBlob = null;

this.INPUT_SIZE = 2048;

this.OUTPUT_SIZE = 32;

this.BATCH_SIZE = 250; // Optimized batch size

}




async init() {

this.Module = await createMintMeModule();

this.inputPtr = this.Module._malloc(this.INPUT_SIZE);

this.outputPtr = this.Module._malloc(this.OUTPUT_SIZE);

this.Module.hash_data = this.Module.cwrap('hash_data', 'void', ['number', 'number', 'number', 'number']);

postMessage({ type: 'ready' });

}




handleMessage(msg) {

if (!this.Module) return;




if (msg.command === 'start') {

if (!this.isMining) {

this.isMining = true;

this._mineLoop();

}

} else if (msg.command === 'job') {

this._setupJob(msg.data);

}

}




_setupJob(jobData) {

this.currentJob = jobData;

// 1. Setup Pristine Blob

let blobHex = this.currentJob.blob.startsWith("0x") ? this.currentJob.blob.slice(2) : this.currentJob.blob;

this.pristineBlob = this._hexToBytes(blobHex);

this.Module.HEAPU8.set(this.pristineBlob, this.inputPtr);




// 2. Setup Time Cost

this.currentTimeCost = 1;

if (this.currentJob.algo && this.currentJob.algo.includes("lyra2-webchain") && !this.currentJob.algo.includes("v2")) {

this.currentTimeCost = 4;

}




// 3. Setup Target (Difficulty Expansion)

if (this.currentJob.target) {

let tHex = this.currentJob.target.startsWith("0x") ? this.currentJob.target.slice(2) : this.currentJob.target;

if (tHex.length <= 8) {

const tmp = BigInt("0x" + this._reverseHex(tHex));

if (tmp > 0n) {

const diff = 0xFFFFFFFFn / tmp;

this.target = diff > 0n ? (0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFn / diff) : 0n;

} else {

this.target = 0n;

}

} else {

this.target = BigInt("0x" + this._reverseHex(tHex));

}

}

// 4. Setup Starting Nonce

this.nonce = BigInt(Math.floor(Math.random() * 0x7FFFFFFF)) << 32n | BigInt(Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xFFFFFFFF));

}




_mineLoop() {

if (!this.isMining || !this.currentJob || !this.pristineBlob) {

setTimeout(() => this._mineLoop(), 100);

return;

}




const view = this.Module.HEAPU8;

const blobLen = this.pristineBlob.length;

const nonceOffset = blobLen - 8;




for (let i = 0; i < this.BATCH_SIZE; i++) {

// Restore pristine memory to prevent C++ mutation pollution

view.set(this.pristineBlob, this.inputPtr);




// Inject 8-byte Little Endian Nonce

for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {

view[this.inputPtr + nonceOffset + b] = Number((this.nonce >> BigInt(b * 8)) & 0xFFn);

}




// Execute WASM Hash

this.Module.hash_data(this.inputPtr, this.outputPtr, blobLen, this.currentTimeCost);




// Check against target

const hashVal = this._getLast64BitsLittleEndian(this.outputPtr);




if (hashVal <= this.target) {

let nHex = "";

for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {

nHex += view[this.inputPtr + nonceOffset + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

}

let resultHex = "";

for (let b = 0; b < 32; b++) {

resultHex += view[this.outputPtr + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

}




postMessage({

type: 'share',

jobId: this.currentJob.job_id,

nonce: nHex,

result: resultHex,

algo: this.currentJob.algo

});

}

this.nonce++;

}

// Yield to event loop to allow new jobs to arrive

setTimeout(() => this._mineLoop(), 0);

}




_hexToBytes(hex) {

const bytes = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);

for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) bytes[i / 2] = parseInt(hex.substr(i, 2), 16);

return bytes;

}




_reverseHex(hex) {

let out = "";

for (let i = hex.length - 2; i >= 0; i -= 2) out += hex.substr(i, 2);

return out;

}




_getLast64BitsLittleEndian(ptr) {

const view = this.Module.HEAPU8;

let hex = "0x";

for (let i = 31; i >= 24; i--) hex += view[ptr + i].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

return BigInt(hex);

}

}




// Instantiate and bind worker messaging

const engine = new WasmMinerEngine();

engine.init();




onmessage = function(e) {

engine.handleMessage(e.data);

};

**Gemini:**
Ah! I see exactly what the pool didn't like. We fell into a classic formatting trap when we modularized the worker ID.

If we look back at the C++ source code from Client.cpp that you shared earlier, this is how the native miner builds the login JSON:

C++
params.AddMember("login", StringRef(m_pool.user()), allocator);
params.AddMember("pass",  StringRef(m_pool.password()), allocator);
params.AddMember("agent", StringRef(m_agent), allocator);

if (m_pool.rigId()) {
    params.AddMember("rigid", StringRef(m_pool.rigId()), allocator);
}


In our MintMeWasmMiner.js, I concatenated the worker name to the wallet address with a dot:
login: "0xa733c9545a3476de410e7704d8be586d59db9c6d.client-checkout"

The Problem: The MintMe pool strictly validates the login field to ensure it is exactly a 42-character hexadecimal wallet address (starting with 0x). When it saw .client-checkout appended to the end, its firewall assumed it was a malformed request or an attack and instantly dropped the TCP connection to protect itself.

The Fix: We must send the pure wallet address in the login field, and use the dedicated rigid (Rig ID) parameter for the worker name, exactly as the C++ native miner does.

Here is the corrected MintMeWasmMiner.js file. (Your mintme_worker.js and checkout.html are still perfectly fine and do not need to be changed!)

Replace your MintMeWasmMiner.js with this:
JavaScript
class MintMeWasmMiner {
    constructor(proxyUrl, walletAddress, workerName = 'web-checkout') {
        this.proxyUrl = proxyUrl;
        this.walletAddress = walletAddress;
        this.workerName = workerName;
        
        this.socket = null;
        this.worker = null;
        this.rpcId = "";
        this.sequenceId = 10;
        this.pendingShares = new Set();
        
        // Callbacks
        this.onProgressCallback = null;
        this.onLog = null;
        this.onHashrate = null;

        // Hashrate tracking
        this.batchHashes = 0;
        this.lastRateUpdate = Date.now();
    }

    setUIHooks(onLogCallback, onHashrateCallback) {
        this.onLog = onLogCallback;
        this.onHashrate = onHashrateCallback;
    }

    _log(msg) {
        console.log(`[Miner] ${msg}`);
        if (this.onLog) this.onLog(msg);
    }

    start(onProgressCallback) {
        this.onProgressCallback = onProgressCallback;
        this._initWorker();
        this._connectWebSocket();
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.socket) {
            this.socket.close();
            this.socket = null;
        }
        if (this.worker) {
            this.worker.terminate();
            this.worker = null;
        }
        this.pendingShares.clear();
        this._log("Stopped successfully.");
        if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate("0.00");
    }

    _initWorker() {
        if (this.worker) return;
        this.worker = new Worker('mintme_worker.js');
        
        this.worker.onmessage = (e) => {
            if (e.data.type === 'share') {
                this._submitShare(e.data.jobId, e.data.nonce, e.data.result, e.data.algo);
            } else if (e.data.type === 'ready') {
                this._log("WASM module ready.");
            } else if (e.data.type === 'stats') {
                this.batchHashes += e.data.hashes;
                const now = Date.now();
                if (now - this.lastRateUpdate >= 1000) {
                    const elapsedSeconds = (now - this.lastRateUpdate) / 1000;
                    const hs = (this.batchHashes / elapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);
                    if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate(hs);
                    this.batchHashes = 0;
                    this.lastRateUpdate = now;
                }
            }
        };
    }

    _connectWebSocket() {
        this.socket = new WebSocket(this.proxyUrl);

        this.socket.onopen = () => {
            this._log(`Connected to pool proxy. Logging in...`);
            
            // CRITICAL FIX: Send pure wallet address in 'login'.
            // Worker ID must be sent as 'rigid' based on C++ source.
            const loginParams = { 
                login: this.walletAddress, 
                pass: "x", 
                agent: "webchain-miner/0.1" 
            };
            
            if (this.workerName) {
                loginParams.rigid = this.workerName;
            }

            this.socket.send(JSON.stringify({
                id: 1,
                jsonrpc: "2.0",
                method: "login",
                params: loginParams
            }));
        };

        this.socket.onmessage = (event) => {
            try {
                const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
                const msgIdNum = parseInt(msg.id);

                if (msg.id === 1 || msg.id === "1") {
                    if (msg.result && msg.result.status === 'OK') {
                        this._log("Login Accepted. Starting mining...");
                        if (msg.result.id) this.rpcId = msg.result.id;
                        if (msg.result.job) this._processJob(msg.result.job);
                    }
                }
                else if (msg.method === 'job') {
                    this._processJob(msg.params);
                }
                else if (this.pendingShares.has(msgIdNum)) {
                    this.pendingShares.delete(msgIdNum);
                    
                    if (msg.result && (msg.result.status === 'OK' || msg.result === true)) {
                        this._log("💰 Share Accepted!");
                        if (this.onProgressCallback) this.onProgressCallback(1); 
                    } else {
                        this._log(`⚠️ Share Rejected.`);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                this._log(`Parse error: ${err}`);
            }
        };
        
        this.socket.onclose = () => {
            this._log("Disconnected from pool proxy.");
        };
    }

    _processJob(jobObj) {
        if (!jobObj || !this.worker) return;
        const shortId = jobObj.job_id ? jobObj.job_id.substring(0, 8) : "Unknown";
        this._log(`📦 New Job received: ${shortId}...`);
        this.worker.postMessage({ command: 'job', data: jobObj });
        this.worker.postMessage({ command: 'start' });
    }

    _submitShare(jobId, nonce, result, algo) {
        this.sequenceId++;
        this.pendingShares.add(this.sequenceId);
        this._log(`💎 Share Found! Nonce: ${nonce}`);

        const submitMessage = {
            id: this.sequenceId,
            jsonrpc: "2.0",
            method: "submit",
            worker: this.walletAddress, // Reverted to pure wallet address
            params: {
                id: this.rpcId,
                job_id: jobId,
                nonce: nonce,   
                result: result, 
                algo: algo || "lyra2v2-webchain" 
            }
        };
        this.socket.send(JSON.stringify(submitMessage));
    }
}


This ensures the walletAddress passes the strict regex on the server, while still securely identifying your worker via the rigid parameter! Try this and it should log in smoothly.

**Gemini:**
This is an excellent transition into the profiling and measurement phase of your project. Since you are drafting a formal manuscript (EBISION2026.tex), having concrete data on hashrates, CPU/RAM overhead, network bandwidth, and power consumption is crucial for proving the viability of your Zero-Liquidity framework.

Here is how we accomplish all four of your goals.

1. Logging Average Hashrate & Hashes Per Share

We need to track totalHashes and the sessionStartTime to calculate the global average. We also need a counter to track hashesSinceLastShare.

Update your MintMeWasmMiner.js file with these specific additions (I have highlighted where the new logic goes):

JavaScript
class MintMeWasmMiner {
    constructor(proxyUrl, walletAddress, workerName = 'web-checkout') {
        // ... (Keep existing properties) ...
        
        // --- NEW STAT TRACKING VARIABLES ---
        this.totalHashes = 0;
        this.sessionStartTime = null;
        this.hashesSinceLastShare = 0; 
    }

    // ... (Keep setUIHooks, _log, start, stop methods as they are) ...
    // Note: Inside stop(), you may want to reset this.sessionStartTime = null;

    _initWorker() {
        if (this.worker) return;
        this.worker = new Worker('mintme_worker.js');
        
        this.worker.onmessage = (e) => {
            if (e.data.type === 'share') {
                this._submitShare(e.data.jobId, e.data.nonce, e.data.result, e.data.algo);
            } else if (e.data.type === 'ready') {
                this._log("WASM module ready.");
            } else if (e.data.type === 'stats') {
                
                // Initialize start time on first hash batch
                if (!this.sessionStartTime) this.sessionStartTime = Date.now();

                // Accumulate stats
                this.batchHashes += e.data.hashes;
                this.totalHashes += e.data.hashes;
                this.hashesSinceLastShare += e.data.hashes;

                const now = Date.now();
                if (now - this.lastRateUpdate >= 1000) {
                    // Instantaneous Hashrate
                    const elapsedSeconds = (now - this.lastRateUpdate) / 1000;
                    const hs = (this.batchHashes / elapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);
                    
                    // Average Hashrate
                    const totalElapsedSeconds = (now - this.sessionStartTime) / 1000;
                    const avgHs = (this.totalHashes / totalElapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);

                    // Send both to the UI
                    if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate(`${hs} (Avg: ${avgHs})`);
                    
                    this.batchHashes = 0;
                    this.lastRateUpdate = now;
                }
            }
        };
    }

    _connectWebSocket() {
        // ... (Keep setup same until the onmessage JSON handler) ...
        
        this.socket.onmessage = (event) => {
            try {
                const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
                const msgIdNum = parseInt(msg.id);

                if (msg.id === 1 || msg.id === "1") {
                    // ... (Keep login handler same) ...
                }
                else if (msg.method === 'job') {
                    // ... (Keep job handler same) ...
                }
                else if (this.pendingShares.has(msgIdNum)) {
                    this.pendingShares.delete(msgIdNum);
                    
                    if (msg.result && (msg.result.status === 'OK' || msg.result === true)) {
                        // --- NEW: LOG THE EFFORT TAKEN TO FIND THIS SHARE ---
                        this._log(`💰 Share Accepted! (Effort: ${this.hashesSinceLastShare} hashes)`);
                        this.hashesSinceLastShare = 0; // Reset counter for the next share
                        
                        if (this.onProgressCallback) this.onProgressCallback(1); 
                    } else {
                        this._log(`⚠️ Share Rejected.`);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                this._log(`Parse error: ${err}`);
            }
        };
        
        // ...
    }
    // ... (Keep the rest of the file the same)
}

2. Monitoring CPU & RAM in Ubuntu 24.04

The best modern tool for this is btop. It provides a beautiful, visually clear breakdown of per-core utilization and memory footprint, which is excellent for taking screenshots for your manuscript.

Install: sudo apt install btop

Run: btop

Why it's good: It will show you exactly which Firefox/Chrome process (your Web Worker) is maxing out a core, and exactly how many Megabytes of RAM your WASM heap (the 2048 bytes + Lyra2 memory) is actually consuming in the browser ecosystem.

3. Monitoring Network Traffic

Mining uses very little bandwidth, but it's highly latency-sensitive. You want to prove in your paper that this doesn't choke the user's internet connection.

Tool: nethogs (Groups bandwidth by the specific process ID).

Install: sudo apt install nethogs

Run: sudo nethogs

Why it's good: It will show you the exact Kilobytes per second (KB/s) being sent and received by your web browser and your Python proxy independently. You will likely see that the stratum protocol only uses a few bytes per minute!

4. Monitoring Electricity / Power Draw

Measuring power draw accurately through software on Linux requires reading the CPU's RAPL (Running Average Power Limit) sensors.

Tool: turbostat (Part of the linux-tools package, standard for academic CPU power measurement).

Install: sudo apt install linux-tools-common linux-tools-generic

Run: sudo turbostat --Summary --quiet --show PkgWatt,CorWatt,Bzy_MHz

Why it's good: PkgWatt tells you the exact wattage being consumed by your entire CPU package.

Methodology for your paper: Measure the PkgWatt while the computer is completely idle. Then, open your checkout.html and start mining. The difference in PkgWatt is the exact electrical cost of your Zero-Liquidity transaction.

(Note: If you are running Ubuntu inside a Virtual Machine on Windows/Mac, software power monitoring will not work. You would need to use a physical "Kill-A-Watt" meter plugged into your computer's power cable).

**Gemini:**
This is the ultimate performance upgrade! By default, JavaScript runs on a single thread. However, modern CPUs have 4, 8, 16, or even more logical cores. By spawning multiple Web Workers (multithreading), you can multiply your hashrate almost linearly based on how many cores you assign to it.

Here is the brilliant part about the way we wrote mintme_worker.js: We don't need to change the worker file at all. Because we programmed the worker to generate a random 64-bit starting nonce (this.nonce = BigInt(...)) whenever it receives a new job, we can safely broadcast the exact same job to 8 different workers. They will all start searching in completely different random areas of the mathematical space, meaning they will never duplicate each other's work!

We simply need to update the main thread to detect the user's CPU cores using navigator.hardwareConcurrency, spawn an array of workers instead of just one, and add a slider to the UI.

Here are the updates.

1. Update MintMeWasmMiner.js (Multithreading Support)

Replace your existing miner class with this version. It changes this.worker into an array, adds a setThreadCount() method, and broadcasts jobs to all active threads.

JavaScript
class MintMeWasmMiner {
    constructor(proxyUrl, walletAddress, workerName = 'web-checkout') {
        this.proxyUrl = proxyUrl;
        this.walletAddress = walletAddress;
        this.workerName = workerName;
        
        this.socket = null;
        this.workers = []; // CHANGED: Now an array of workers
        this.threadCount = 1; // Default to 1 thread
        this.rpcId = "";
        this.sequenceId = 10;
        this.pendingShares = new Set();
        
        // Callbacks
        this.onProgressCallback = null;
        this.onLog = null;
        this.onHashrate = null;

        // Hashrate tracking
        this.batchHashes = 0;
        this.totalHashes = 0;
        this.sessionStartTime = null;
        this.hashesSinceLastShare = 0; 
        this.lastRateUpdate = Date.now();
    }

    setUIHooks(onLogCallback, onHashrateCallback) {
        this.onLog = onLogCallback;
        this.onHashrate = onHashrateCallback;
    }

    // NEW: Allow UI to configure threads before starting
    setThreadCount(count) {
        this.threadCount = count;
    }

    _log(msg) {
        console.log(`[Miner] ${msg}`);
        if (this.onLog) this.onLog(msg);
    }

    start(onProgressCallback) {
        this.onProgressCallback = onProgressCallback;
        this.sessionStartTime = null; // Reset session timer
        this._initWorkers(); // CHANGED: Call the plural init
        this._connectWebSocket();
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.socket) {
            this.socket.close();
            this.socket = null;
        }
        // CHANGED: Terminate all workers
        if (this.workers.length > 0) {
            this.workers.forEach(w => w.terminate());
            this.workers = [];
        }
        this.pendingShares.clear();
        this._log("Stopped successfully.");
        if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate("0.00");
    }

    _initWorkers() {
        this._log(`Initializing ${this.threadCount} Web Workers...`);
        for (let i = 0; i < this.threadCount; i++) {
            const worker = new Worker('mintme_worker.js');
            
            worker.onmessage = (e) => {
                if (e.data.type === 'share') {
                    this._submitShare(e.data.jobId, e.data.nonce, e.data.result, e.data.algo);
                } else if (e.data.type === 'ready') {
                    this._log(`WASM module ready on thread ${i + 1}.`);
                } else if (e.data.type === 'stats') {
                    // Aggregate stats from all threads
                    if (!this.sessionStartTime) this.sessionStartTime = Date.now();

                    this.batchHashes += e.data.hashes;
                    this.totalHashes += e.data.hashes;
                    this.hashesSinceLastShare += e.data.hashes;

                    const now = Date.now();
                    if (now - this.lastRateUpdate >= 1000) {
                        const elapsedSeconds = (now - this.lastRateUpdate) / 1000;
                        const hs = (this.batchHashes / elapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);
                        
                        const totalElapsedSeconds = (now - this.sessionStartTime) / 1000;
                        const avgHs = (this.totalHashes / totalElapsedSeconds).toFixed(2);

                        if (this.onHashrate) this.onHashrate(`${hs} (Avg: ${avgHs})`);
                        
                        this.batchHashes = 0;
                        this.lastRateUpdate = now;
                    }
                }
            };
            this.workers.push(worker);
        }
    }

    _connectWebSocket() {
        this.socket = new WebSocket(this.proxyUrl);

        this.socket.onopen = () => {
            this._log(`Connected to pool proxy. Logging in...`);
            
            const loginParams = { 
                login: this.walletAddress, 
                pass: "x", 
                agent: "webchain-miner/0.1" 
            };
            
            if (this.workerName) {
                loginParams.rigid = this.workerName;
            }

            this.socket.send(JSON.stringify({
                id: 1,
                jsonrpc: "2.0",
                method: "login",
                params: loginParams
            }));
        };

        this.socket.onmessage = (event) => {
            try {
                const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
                const msgIdNum = parseInt(msg.id);

                if (msg.id === 1 || msg.id === "1") {
                    if (msg.result && msg.result.status === 'OK') {
                        this._log("Login Accepted. Starting mining...");
                        if (msg.result.id) this.rpcId = msg.result.id;
                        if (msg.result.job) this._processJob(msg.result.job);
                    }
                }
                else if (msg.method === 'job') {
                    this._processJob(msg.params);
                }
                else if (this.pendingShares.has(msgIdNum)) {
                    this.pendingShares.delete(msgIdNum);
                    
                    if (msg.result && (msg.result.status === 'OK' || msg.result === true)) {
                        this._log(`💰 Share Accepted! (Effort: ${this.hashesSinceLastShare} hashes)`);
                        this.hashesSinceLastShare = 0;
                        if (this.onProgressCallback) this.onProgressCallback(1); 
                    } else {
                        this._log(`⚠️ Share Rejected.`);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                this._log(`Parse error: ${err}`);
            }
        };
        
        this.socket.onclose = () => {
            this._log("Disconnected from pool proxy.");
        };
    }

    _processJob(jobObj) {
        if (!jobObj || this.workers.length === 0) return;
        const shortId = jobObj.job_id ? jobObj.job_id.substring(0, 8) : "Unknown";
        this._log(`📦 New Job: ${shortId}... Broadcasting to threads.`);
        
        // CHANGED: Broadcast the job and start command to EVERY worker
        this.workers.forEach(w => {
            w.postMessage({ command: 'job', data: jobObj });
            w.postMessage({ command: 'start' });
        });
    }

    _submitShare(jobId, nonce, result, algo) {
        this.sequenceId++;
        this.pendingShares.add(this.sequenceId);
        this._log(`💎 Share Found! Nonce: ${nonce}`);

        const submitMessage = {
            id: this.sequenceId,
            jsonrpc: "2.0",
            method: "submit",
            worker: this.walletAddress,
            params: {
                id: this.rpcId,
                job_id: jobId,
                nonce: nonce,   
                result: result, 
                algo: algo || "lyra2v2-webchain" 
            }
        };
        this.socket.send(JSON.stringify(submitMessage));
    }
}

I have added an HTML range slider to let the user select how many CPU threads they want to dedicate to the transaction. It automatically detects their maximum logical cores using navigator.hardwareConcurrency and dynamically adjusts the maximum limit.

HTML
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Zero-Liquidity Premium Content</title>

        .progress-bar-container { background: #eee; border-radius: 20px; width: 100%; height: 25px; margin: 20px 0; overflow: hidden; }
        .progress-bar { background: #4CAF50; height: 100%; width: 0%; transition: width 0.3s; }

        button:hover { background: #0056b3; }
        button:disabled { background: #ccc; cursor: not-allowed; }

        .feedback-panel { background: #1a1a1a; border-radius: 5px; padding: 15px; text-align: left; }
        .hashrate-display { color: #0ff; font-family: 'Consolas', monospace; font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.1em; margin-bottom: 10px; display: block; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; padding-bottom: 10px;}
        .log-box { height: 120px; overflow-y: scroll; font-family: 'Consolas', monospace; font-size: 0.8em; color: #888; }
        .log-box div { margin-bottom: 4px; }

        /* New Slider Styles */
        .thread-slider-container { margin: 15px 0; text-align: left; }
        .thread-slider-container label { font-size: 0.9em; font-weight: bold; color: #555; }
        input[type=range] { width: 100%; margin-top: 8px; }
    </style>
    <script src="MintMeWasmMiner.js"></script>
</head>
<body>

    <div class="checkout-box">
        <h2>Unlock Premium E-Book</h2>
        <p style="color: #666; font-size: 0.9em;">Price: 10 Computed Shares</p>
        <p style="color: #888; font-size: 0.8em;">No credit card required. Keep this tab open to attest your device's computation.</p>

        <div class="thread-slider-container">
            <label for="threadSlider">CPU Threads Dedicated: <span id="threadCountDisplay" style="color: #007BFF;">1</span></label>
            <input type="range" id="threadSlider" min="1" max="4" value="1" oninput="document.getElementById('threadCountDisplay').innerText = this.value;">
        </div>

        <div class="progress-bar-container">
            <div id="paymentProgress" class="progress-bar"></div>
        </div>
        <p id="statusText" style="font-weight: bold;">0 / 10 Shares Mined</p>

        <button id="btnCheckout" onclick="initiateCheckout()">Start Computational Payment</button>

        <div class="feedback-panel">
            <span class="hashrate-display">Speed: <span id="uiHashrate">0.00</span> H/s</span>
            <div id="uiLogBox" class="log-box">
                <div>[System] Waiting to start...</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // --- CPU Detection Logic ---
        const maxThreads = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4; // Fallback to 4 if API blocked
        const threadSlider = document.getElementById('threadSlider');
        threadSlider.max = maxThreads;
        
        // Default to half of available cores, but at least 1
        const defaultThreads = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxThreads / 2));
        threadSlider.value = defaultThreads;
        document.getElementById('threadCountDisplay').innerText = defaultThreads;


        // 1. Define the Business Logic Controller
        class ZeroLiquidityCheckout {
            constructor(config) {
                this.targetPrice = config.targetPrice;
                this.payment = 0;
                this.pricingMetric = config.pricingMetric; 
                this.mainMining = config.mainMining; 
                this.mainItemDelivery = config.mainItemDelivery;
            }

            startPayment() {
                this.mainMining.start((minedAmount) => {
                    this.monitorProgress(minedAmount);
                });
            }

            monitorProgress(minedAmount) {
                this.payment += minedAmount;
                const percentage = Math.min((this.payment / this.targetPrice) * 100, 100);
                document.getElementById('paymentProgress').style.width = percentage + "%";
                document.getElementById('statusText').innerText = `${this.payment} / ${this.targetPrice} Shares Mined`;

                if (this.payment >= this.targetPrice) {
                    this.mainMining.stop();            
                    this.mainItemDelivery.execute();  
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Define Delivery Mechanism
        class FileUnlockDelivery {
            constructor(filename) {
                this.filename = filename;
            }
            execute() {
                document.getElementById('btnCheckout').innerText = "Download Unlocked!";
                document.getElementById('btnCheckout').style.background = "#28a745";
                document.getElementById('statusText').innerText = "Payment Complete!";
                alert(`Success! Delivery executed for: ${this.filename}`);
            }
        }

        // 3. Initialize Mining Engine & Bind UI Hooks
        const minerEngine = new MintMeWasmMiner(
            'ws://127.0.0.1:8888', 
            '0xa733c9545a3476de410e7704d8be586d59db9c6d', 
            'client-checkout'
        );

        minerEngine.setUIHooks(
            (logMessage) => {
                const el = document.getElementById('uiLogBox');
                const d = document.createElement('div');
                d.innerText = `[${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}] ${logMessage}`;
                el.prepend(d); 
            },
            (hashrate) => {
                document.getElementById('uiHashrate').innerText = hashrate;
            }
        );

        const myCheckout = new ZeroLiquidityCheckout({
            targetPrice: 10, 
            pricingMetric: "shares",
            mainMining: minerEngine, 
            mainItemDelivery: new FileUnlockDelivery('premium_file.pdf') 
        });

        // UI Trigger
        function initiateCheckout() {
            // Lock the thread slider and button so user can't change it while mining
            document.getElementById('btnCheckout').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('threadSlider').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('btnCheckout').innerText = "Attesting Computation...";
            
            // Pass the user's thread choice into the engine
            const selectedThreads = parseInt(document.getElementById('threadSlider').value);
            minerEngine.setThreadCount(selectedThreads);
            
            myCheckout.startPayment();
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

**Gemini:**
Ah, good catch! I see exactly what happened. This was a slight oversight on my part when we converted the worker.js logic into the modular WasmMinerEngine class.

When I rewrote the _mineLoop() method inside the class, I accidentally deleted the single line of code that sends the stats message back to the main thread after every batch. Because the worker wasn't reporting its progress, the main thread's counters (totalHashes, batchHashes, and hashesSinceLastShare) just sat at 0 forever, even though the worker was successfully hashing and finding shares in the background!

The Fix

Open your mintme_worker.js file, scroll down to the bottom of the _mineLoop() method, and add the missing postMessage line right after the for loop finishes.

Here is the corrected _mineLoop() function:

JavaScript
    _mineLoop() {
        if (!this.isMining || !this.currentJob || !this.pristineBlob) {
            setTimeout(() => this._mineLoop(), 100);
            return;
        }

        const view = this.Module.HEAPU8;
        const blobLen = this.pristineBlob.length;
        const nonceOffset = blobLen - 8; 

        for (let i = 0; i < this.BATCH_SIZE; i++) {
            // Restore pristine memory to prevent C++ mutation pollution
            view.set(this.pristineBlob, this.inputPtr);

            // Inject 8-byte Little Endian Nonce
            for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {
                view[this.inputPtr + nonceOffset + b] = Number((this.nonce >> BigInt(b * 8)) & 0xFFn);
            }

            // Execute WASM Hash
            this.Module.hash_data(this.inputPtr, this.outputPtr, blobLen, this.currentTimeCost);

            // Check against target
            const hashVal = this._getLast64BitsLittleEndian(this.outputPtr);

            if (hashVal <= this.target) {
                let nHex = "";
                for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {
                    nHex += view[this.inputPtr + nonceOffset + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
                }
                
                let resultHex = "";
                for (let b = 0; b < 32; b++) {
                    resultHex += view[this.outputPtr + b].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
                }

                postMessage({
                    type: 'share',
                    jobId: this.currentJob.job_id,
                    nonce: nHex,
                    result: resultHex,
                    algo: this.currentJob.algo
                });
            }
            this.nonce++;
        }
        
        // --- CRITICAL FIX: SEND STATS TO MAIN THREAD ---
        postMessage({ type: 'stats', hashes: this.BATCH_SIZE });
        
        // Yield to event loop to allow new jobs to arrive
        setTimeout(() => this._mineLoop(), 0); 
    }


Add that single postMessage line, and your UI will instantly light up with the actual hashrate (which matches the ~48.00 H/s you are seeing on the MintMe dashboard) and the accurate "Effort" calculation!

**Gemini:**
This is a crucial addition. Ethical transparency is the cornerstone of "Zero-Liquidity" web mining. Without explicit consent and hardware warnings, web mining is often flagged as "cryptojacking" by antivirus software.

Adding the Proof-of-Consent pop-up and the "Pause" functionality requires changes strictly to the checkout.html file. The underlying MintMeWasmMiner.js engine already supports stopping and starting perfectly, so we just need to wire the UI to it!

Here is the fully updated checkout.html. I have added the modal (pop-up) HTML, the CSS to make it look professional and center it on the screen, and the JavaScript state logic to handle Start, Pause, and Resume.

Replace your checkout.html with this:
HTML
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Zero-Liquidity Premium Content</title>
    <style>
        body { font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif; background: #f4f4f9; padding: 40px; text-align: center; }
        .checkout-box { background: #fff; border-radius: 10px; padding: 30px; max-width: 450px; margin: 0 auto; box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); position: relative; }
        h2 { margin-top: 0; color: #333; }
        
        .progress-bar-container { background: #eee; border-radius: 20px; width: 100%; height: 25px; margin: 20px 0; overflow: hidden; }
        .progress-bar { background: #4CAF50; height: 100%; width: 0%; transition: width 0.3s; }
        
        button { background: #007BFF; color: #fff; border: none; padding: 12px 25px; border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 1em; font-weight: bold; width: 100%; margin-bottom: 20px; transition: background 0.2s;}
        button:hover { background: #0056b3; }
        button:disabled { background: #ccc !important; cursor: not-allowed; }
        
        /* Dynamic Button States */
        .btn-pause { background: #ff9800 !important; }
        .btn-pause:hover { background: #e68a00 !important; }
        .btn-success { background: #28a745 !important; }

        .feedback-panel { background: #1a1a1a; border-radius: 5px; padding: 15px; text-align: left; }
        .hashrate-display { color: #0ff; font-family: 'Consolas', monospace; font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.1em; margin-bottom: 10px; display: block; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; padding-bottom: 10px;}
        .log-box { height: 120px; overflow-y: scroll; font-family: 'Consolas', monospace; font-size: 0.8em; color: #888; }
        .log-box div { margin-bottom: 4px; }

        .thread-slider-container { margin: 15px 0; text-align: left; }
        .thread-slider-container label { font-size: 0.9em; font-weight: bold; color: #555; }
        input[type=range] { width: 100%; margin-top: 8px; }

        /* --- NEW MODAL (POP-UP) STYLES --- */
        .modal-overlay { display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); z-index: 1000; justify-content: center; align-items: center; }
        .modal-content { background: #fff; padding: 25px; border-radius: 8px; max-width: 500px; text-align: left; box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); }
        .modal-content h3 { margin-top: 0; color: #d32f2f; }
        .warning-box { background: #ffebee; border-left: 4px solid #f44336; padding: 10px; margin: 15px 0; font-size: 0.9em; color: #c62828; }
        .transparency-box { background: #f5f5f5; border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px; font-size: 0.85em; color: #555; font-family: monospace; margin-bottom: 20px;}
        .modal-buttons { display: flex; gap: 10px; }
        .btn-consent { background: #4CAF50; flex: 1; }
        .btn-consent:hover { background: #388E3C; }
        .btn-decline { background: #9e9e9e; flex: 1; }
        .btn-decline:hover { background: #757575; }
    </style>
    <script src="MintMeWasmMiner.js"></script>
</head>
<body>

    <div id="consentModal" class="modal-overlay">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <h3>Computation Consent Required</h3>
            <p style="font-size: 0.95em; color: #333;">To access this content without a fiat payment, this website requests permission to use a portion of your device's processing power (CPU) to perform cryptographic calculations.</p>
            
            <div class="warning-box">
                <strong>Hardware Impact Warning:</strong><br>
                Running this process will utilize your processor. On mobile devices or laptops, this may result in increased battery drain, higher electricity consumption, and increased device temperature. You have full control and can adjust the processing intensity (thread count) or stop the process entirely at any time.
            </div>

            <div class="transparency-box">
                <strong>Transparency Details:</strong><br><br>
                <strong>Action:</strong> Attesting computational shares to unlock content.<br>
                <strong>Network:</strong> MintMe (Lyra2v2 Webchain)<br>
                <strong>Pool:</strong> pool.mintme.com:2222<br>
                <strong>Destination:</strong> 0xa733c9545a3476de410e7704d8be586d59db9c6d
            </div>

            <div class="modal-buttons">
                <button class="btn-consent" onclick="acceptConsent()">I Understand and Consent</button>
                <button class="btn-decline" onclick="closeConsentModal()">Decline and Go Back</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="checkout-box">
        <h2>Unlock Premium E-Book</h2>
        <p style="color: #666; font-size: 0.9em;">Price: 10 Computed Shares</p>
        <p style="color: #888; font-size: 0.8em;">No credit card required. Keep this tab open to attest your device's computation.</p>

        <div class="thread-slider-container">
            <label for="threadSlider">CPU Threads Dedicated: <span id="threadCountDisplay" style="color: #007BFF;">1</span></label>
            <input type="range" id="threadSlider" min="1" max="4" value="1" oninput="document.getElementById('threadCountDisplay').innerText = this.value;">
        </div>

        <div class="progress-bar-container">
            <div id="paymentProgress" class="progress-bar"></div>
        </div>
        <p id="statusText" style="font-weight: bold;">0 / 10 Shares Mined</p>

        <button id="btnCheckout" onclick="handleMainButton()">Start Computational Payment</button>

        <div class="feedback-panel">
            <span class="hashrate-display">Speed: <span id="uiHashrate">0.00</span> H/s</span>
            <div id="uiLogBox" class="log-box">
                <div>[System] Waiting to start...</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // Thread setup
        const maxThreads = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4;
        const threadSlider = document.getElementById('threadSlider');
        threadSlider.max = maxThreads;
        const defaultThreads = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxThreads / 2));
        threadSlider.value = defaultThreads;
        document.getElementById('threadCountDisplay').innerText = defaultThreads;

        // UI State Variables
        let minerState = "STOPPED"; // STOPPED, RUNNING, PAUSED, COMPLETE

        // 1. Business Logic Controller (Added Pause capability)
        class ZeroLiquidityCheckout {
            constructor(config) {
                this.targetPrice = config.targetPrice;
                this.payment = 0;
                this.mainMining = config.mainMining; 
                this.mainItemDelivery = config.mainItemDelivery;
            }

            startPayment() {
                this.mainMining.start((minedAmount) => {
                    this.monitorProgress(minedAmount);
                });
            }

            pausePayment() {
                this.mainMining.stop();
            }

            monitorProgress(minedAmount) {
                this.payment += minedAmount;
                const percentage = Math.min((this.payment / this.targetPrice) * 100, 100);
                document.getElementById('paymentProgress').style.width = percentage + "%";
                document.getElementById('statusText').innerText = `${this.payment} / ${this.targetPrice} Shares Mined`;

                if (this.payment >= this.targetPrice) {
                    minerState = "COMPLETE";
                    this.mainMining.stop();            
                    this.mainItemDelivery.execute();  
                }
            }
        }

        class FileUnlockDelivery {
            constructor(filename) { this.filename = filename; }
            execute() {
                const btn = document.getElementById('btnCheckout');
                btn.disabled = true;
                btn.className = "btn-success";
                btn.innerText = "Download Unlocked!";
                document.getElementById('statusText').innerText = "Payment Complete!";
                document.getElementById('threadSlider').disabled = true;
                alert(`Success! Delivery executed for: ${this.filename}`);
            }
        }

        const minerEngine = new MintMeWasmMiner(
            'ws://127.0.0.1:8888', 
            '0xa733c9545a3476de410e7704d8be586d59db9c6d', 
            'client-checkout'
        );

        minerEngine.setUIHooks(
            (logMessage) => {
                const el = document.getElementById('uiLogBox');
                const d = document.createElement('div');
                d.innerText = `[${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}] ${logMessage}`;
                el.prepend(d); 
            },
            (hashrate) => { document.getElementById('uiHashrate').innerText = hashrate; }
        );

        const myCheckout = new ZeroLiquidityCheckout({
            targetPrice: 10, 
            mainMining: minerEngine, 
            mainItemDelivery: new FileUnlockDelivery('premium_file.pdf') 
        });

        // --- NEW UI EVENT HANDLERS ---

        function handleMainButton() {
            if (minerState === "STOPPED") {
                // First time clicking: Show consent pop-up
                document.getElementById('consentModal').style.display = "flex";
            } else if (minerState === "RUNNING") {
                // Currently mining: Pause it
                pauseMining();
            } else if (minerState === "PAUSED") {
                // Currently paused: Resume it
                resumeMining();
            }
        }

        function closeConsentModal() {
            document.getElementById('consentModal').style.display = "none";
        }

        function acceptConsent() {
            closeConsentModal();
            startMining();
        }

        function startMining() {
            minerState = "RUNNING";
            
            const btn = document.getElementById('btnCheckout');
            btn.className = "btn-pause";
            btn.innerText = "Pause Computation";
            
            document.getElementById('threadSlider').disabled = true;
            const selectedThreads = parseInt(document.getElementById('threadSlider').value);
            minerEngine.setThreadCount(selectedThreads);
            
            myCheckout.startPayment();
        }

        function pauseMining() {
            minerState = "PAUSED";
            myCheckout.pausePayment();
            
            const btn = document.getElementById('btnCheckout');
            btn.className = ""; // Remove pause styling, revert to default blue
            btn.innerText = "Resume Computation";
            
            // Allow user to change thread count while paused
            document.getElementById('threadSlider').disabled = false; 
            
            const el = document.getElementById('uiLogBox');
            const d = document.createElement('div');
            d.innerText = `[${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}] [System] Computation Paused by User.`;
            d.style.color = "#ff9800";
            el.prepend(d);
        }

        function resumeMining() {
            startMining(); // Logic is identical to starting, it just picks up where payment left off
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

What this accomplishes:

The Pop-Up (<div id="consentModal">): Uses a classic high-z-index flexbox overlay to blur out the background and command attention.

State Machine: I introduced minerState (STOPPED, RUNNING, PAUSED, COMPLETE) to manage what the main button does.

Dynamic Thread Management: When paused, the thread slider is automatically unlocked again, allowing the user to lower the intensity if their laptop got too hot, and then hit Resume to continue with fewer threads.
